# ZenaQuiet-Headset Status Report — 2026-11-03

Source: live Jira, project `HS` (ZenaQuiet-Headset), snapshot `data/ZenaQuiet-Headset/2026-11-03.json` (556 issues), plus `raw/threads/` and `raw/transcripts/`. Stage windows and lead times from [[reference#Stage schedule (per product)]] and [[reference#Purchasing lead times]]. Previous report: [[ZenaQuiet-Headset/jira/2026-11-02-jira|2026-11-02]]. Rows not shown in sections 3 and 5 are in the Appendix at the end.

**Since last report:** Compared with snapshot 2026-11-02: 556 → 556 issues; Added: 0; Removed: 0; Status changes: 0; Date changes: 0; Title changes: 0; Assignee changes: 0; Links added: 0; Links removed: 0.

This report follows yesterday's ([[ZenaQuiet-Headset/jira/2026-11-02-jira|2026-11-02]]) by one day, so most of it repeats that report with the dates moved on.

- **Nothing changed in Jira since 2026-11-02.** A field-by-field comparison of all 556 issues (titles, statuses, dates, sprints, owners, links, descriptions, comments and "last updated" times) found no difference. No issue has been touched since the 2026-11-02 pull. There are no new comments (still 26), so Jira records nothing yet from the CTO visit that started 2026-11-02. Jira also shows no PCB (printed circuit board) payment and no chip decision on HS-164.
- **Moved only because a day passed:** the Headset PCB forecast moves from 2026-11-23 to 2026-11-24 and is now 46 working days late (source risk). HS-330 (ID step 4/6, owner Chico Ly) is 4 days late (was 3). The gate is 97 days away.
- **Stale count jumped from 99 to 504, as the 2026-11-02 report expected.** The 405 open items last touched by the bulk title edit on 2026-10-20 crossed the 14-day "no update" line today. That raises LOW risks from 77 to 481. This is a timing effect, not new slippage.
- **Still open from last week:** 482 of 516 open items have no owner (7 Epics, 75 Stories, 400 Subtasks). The EE (electronics) Headset tickets promised on 2026-10-19 are still missing. The 51 tickets in POC Sprint 3 (ended 2026-11-01) have not been moved.

Jira alone is AMBER (9 HIGH Epic-date risks, none on the gate). Readiness is 33.3%, ahead of the 30.7% expected, but 2 of the 8 Done Stories are "[TO BE REMOVED]" Arc Stories.

### Top 3 risks

**1. Headset PCBs still held at the supplier, now 15 days**

- **What:** The paid order used the wrong board option, and nothing shows the ~$35 extra has been paid.
- **Tickets:**
  - [HS-144](https://epazz.atlassian.net/browse/HS-144) — [POC] Basic Single Boom Mic Hardware Setup and Raw Audio Capture (needs the boards, inferred)
  - [HS-162](https://epazz.atlassian.net/browse/HS-162) — Port minimal I2S audio driver (firmware waits on the board, inferred)

- **Impact:** Boards arrive about 2026-11-24 at best; each unpaid week adds five working days.

**2. Chip choice still not recorded while Research proposes a different chip**

- **What:** Jira still plans the NXP chip; Research dropped XMOS and proposed a Rockchip chip on 2026-10-23.
- **Tickets:**
  - [HS-164](https://epazz.atlassian.net/browse/HS-164) — [POC] SoC Selection, Analysis, and EER Data Collection (still names NXP i.MX 95; its sprint ended 2026-11-01)

- **Impact:** A chip change reworks the board, power and firmware plan; core POC work stays unstarted.

**3. Arc noise target missed again; scope still undecided**

- **What:** The latest print test says the Arc cannot reach 8–12 dB without power, the same as the Halo.
- **Tickets:**

  - [HS-89](https://epazz.atlassian.net/browse/HS-89) — [POC] Arc Magnetic Latch Force Validation, [TO BE REMOVED] (In Process)
- **Impact:** Mechanical time keeps going to a feature whose main target the tests do not support.

## 2. Product Detail

**Purpose:** A headset for call-centre (BPO) agents who wear it for 8–12 hours a shift. It captures the agent's voice cleanly for the Voice Lock speech pipeline (HS-1) and cancels room noise at the ears (HS-2). A clip-on magnetic Arc is meant to reduce how much neighbours overhear (HS-51). It connects over USB-C and Bluetooth to Zoo Meet and the ZenaAI cloud (HS-380, HS-451). No Epic title, owner, date or description changed since 2026-11-02.

**Technical requirements by Epic:**

- HS-1 ACOUSTIC LAYER 3_Inbound Audio Capture at Headset's Microphone (due 2027-09-06, 0% done, variance 0d, To Do)
  - Boom mic array of 4 MEMS (tiny chip) digital mics with beam-forming, which aims the mics at the agent's mouth. Voice goes to the Voice Lock TSE model (target speaker extraction: it keeps only the enrolled agent's voice) with EER (equal error rate) ≤1.5% [EE, FW, AC (inferred)]
  - POC: single boom mic capture, voice detection and noise suppression firmware, chip selection (HS-144, HS-161, HS-164), none started [ME, EE, FW]. Jira plans the NXP i.MX 95 chip (HS-154, HS-155, HS-162, HS-165, HS-166) [EE, FW]
  - Later phases: single-mic SNR (signal-to-noise ratio) ≥25 dB at 300–700 mm; voice detection false positive ≤2%, false negative ≤1% [FW]
- HS-2 ACOUSTIC LAYER 2_Outbound Audio Output at Agent's Ears With Active Noise Cancelling (due 2027-09-06, 0% done, variance 0d, To Do)
  - Hybrid ANC (active noise cancelling using outer and inner mics): ≥25 dB cancellation from 20 Hz to 1 kHz, ≤1.5 ms delay, no feedback squeal [EE, FW, AC (inferred)]
  - POC: earcup passive isolation baseline (HS-13) and first feedback ANC stability test (HS-48), both not started [ME, EE, FW]
  - The description states the team has no documented ANC experience, so the EVT-2 targets need a specialist or consultant [AC, FW]
- HS-3 Power Supply & Battery Management & Fast Charging (due 2027-09-06, 17% done, variance 0d, To Do)
  - At least 8 h of wireless use at full load; 1,200 mAh Li-Po battery; ≥500 charge cycles at ≥80% capacity [EE]
  - USB-C fast charge: 0–50% in ≤20 min (HS-422, not started), 0–100% in ≤60 min [EE, FW, SW]
  - Battery management chip, power rails, battery safety certification, factory end-of-line battery test [EE, FA (inferred)]
- HS-4 User Interface (Control & Status) (due 2027-09-06, 0% done, variance -105d, To Do)
  - Buttons for mute, ANC, volume and the Zena AI assistant; power/pairing slider; 360° Voice Lock LED (HS-117, HS-118) [ME, EE, FW, SW]
  - All controls respond within 200 ms, can be used by touch alone, and still work after the IP54 sweat test [FW, ME]
  - LED positions moved to EVT (the first engineering-prototype stage); EE adds an LED pad and socket on the PCB (HS-301) [ID, EE]
- HS-6 Mechanical Structure (Product Enclosure) (due 2027-09-06, 18% done, variance 0d, In Progress)
  - IP54 (dust and splash resistant); survives a 1.5 m drop without the Arc coming off; 10,000 Arc attach/detach cycles; ≤180 g without the Arc [ME]
  - Battery isolation and puncture protection next to the AI chip [ME, EE]
  - POC: headband fit (HS-580 In Testing; HS-586 Done), clamping force 4–8 N (HS-584, due 2026-11-16), fold hinge modelled in Solidworks (HS-588) [ME]
  - EVT-1 earcup and headband model (HS-128): model update HS-335 Done, EE size inputs HS-336 and HS-337 not started [ME, EE]
- HS-47 stop creating trash and assign to me (due 2027-09-06, 0% done, variance n/a, To Do)
  - No description in Jira and no child Stories; nothing to infer.
- HS-51 ACOUSTIC LAYER 1_ARC Directional Masking (NOT THE SAME AS PRO's SOUND MASKING) (due 2027-09-06, 10% done, variance 0d, To Do)
  - A magnetic, detachable Arc on the boom arm plays a masking sound at head height (0.3–0.8 m) so neighbours overhear less [ME, EE, AC (inferred)]
  - Passive deflection should cut outward speech by 8–12 dB at 0.5 m. The 2026-10-27 print test says it cannot (HS-79) [ME, AC]
  - POC Stories are all titled "[TO BE REMOVED]": HS-86 and HS-127 (via HS-6) Done; HS-78 and HS-89 In Process; HS-93 latch test due 2026-12-01 [ME]
- HS-142 HEADSET Industrial Design (due 2027-02-08, 25% done, variance +210d, To Do)
  - Outer shape of the headset and Arc in three rounds: form model (HS-318 Done), CMF lock (colour, material, finish; HS-320), pre-DVT geometry sign-off (HS-323) [ID, ME]
  - Six-step design process. Step 4/6 "Prototyping Shape" (HS-330) was due 2026-10-30 and is 4 days late [ID]
- HS-380 ACOUSTIC LAYER 4_Zoo Meet Integration & ZenaAI Cloud Denoise (due 2027-09-06, 0% done, variance 0d, To Do)
  - Shows up as a USB and Bluetooth audio device in Zoo Meet; ≤15 ms end-to-end delay (HS-381, HS-385) [FW, SW, EE]
  - Voice Lock status and ANC controls in the Zoo Meet screen; remote firmware updates [SW, FW]
  - Falls back to ZenaAI cloud denoise when on-headset processing is unavailable [SW]
- HS-451 Connectivity & Ports (due 2027-09-06, 0% done, variance 0d, To Do)
  - USB-C on the right earcup: USB audio, at least 18 W fast charge, Zoo Meet control (HS-452) [EE, FW, SW, ME]
  - Bluetooth 5.3 multipoint (connected to two devices at once), several codecs, ≥20 m range despite metal parts (HS-457) [EE, FW]
  - 5-pin magnetic connector to the Arc; FCC and CE certification [EE, ME]
- HS-640 Field Test+RC (due 2027-02-08, 0% done, variance +210d, To Do)
  - Field-test plan, end-to-end function, audio quality, consent and voiceprint erasure, battery, firmware update and rollback, Go/No-Go report (HS-641 to HS-647, 2027-05-24 → 2027-09-06) [FW, SW (inferred)]
  - The scope text still names Beacon and Pro builds, so it looks copied from another product's Epic (inferred)

**Engineering discussion (from Jira comments):**

I read all 26 HS comments; none is new since 2026-11-02. Only one falls in the last 14 days (2026-10-20 to 2026-11-03): HS-79 by Nguyen Trung Tin. The HS-301 comment (2026-10-19) is included below because it explains an open item. No comment mentions the PCB order or payment, the chip choice, or the CTO visit that started 2026-11-02.

- **HS-51 Arc Directional Masking**
  - Being discussed: whether the Arc can block speech without power. The printed Arc geometry was finished (HS-79, Nguyen Trung Tin, 2026-10-27).
  - Thinking so far: "Like HALO Arc, it cannot passively meet the target of 8–12 dB outward bleed reduction and NRC ≥0.70 at 1 kHz with the dimensions specified in TPS" (HS-79, Nguyen Trung Tin, 2026-10-27). NRC (noise reduction coefficient) measures how much sound a surface absorbs. This repeats the 1–2 dB result from the 2026-09-30 meeting (source risk).
  - Decided: nothing on scope. HS-79 was closed as Done even though the target was missed.
  - Still open: whether the Arc is dropped, made active (powered), or kept with a lower target. HS-51 still says 8–12 dB, and HS-78 and HS-89 are still In Process (waiting on Cang Le and the CTO, inferred).
- **HS-4 User Interface**
  - Decided: LED positions move to the EVT phase (owner Chico Ly). EE (Phong.ngoc.nguyen) adds an LED pad and socket on the PCB (HS-301, Nguyen Trung Tin, 2026-10-19).
  - Still open: HS-301 is Done but still in a POC sprint (see Data Hygiene).
- **HS-6 Mechanical Structure / HS-1 boom mic** (older comment, still open)
  - Still open: Acoustic was asked to recommend mic PCB locations, and EE to design a mic PCB with adjustable spacing (HS-148, Nguyen Trung Tin, 2026-10-12). There is no answer in Jira (waiting on Acoustic and EE).

**Outside HS:** the chip question is moving in the Research project only. Research ruled out XMOS for the AI model (RS-1, Nguyen Binh Minh, 2026-10-22) and proposed the Rockchip RK3576, asking for an evaluation kit (RS-2, Nguyen Binh Minh, 2026-10-23). There is no newer RS comment in today's Research pull, those comments name no product, and no HS ticket links to them. `comment_signals.json` has one entry: the HS-79 test result.

- **HS-641 to HS-647 (HIGH, R4 + R6).** Seven field-test Stories (2027-05-24 → 2027-09-06) sit under Epic HS-640, which ends 2027-02-08, so on paper the Epic slips at least 210 days. This is DVT work with no POC gate impact. FW and SW wait (inferred). No change since 2026-10-26.
- **HS-330 (MEDIUM, R1 + R4).** ID step 4/6 "Prototyping Shape" is 4 days past its 2026-10-30 due date and now also stale (14 days). It holds up HS-326 (form model) → HS-142. There are 101 days of buffer, so the gate is not threatened. Owner Chico Ly.
- **HS-144, HS-161, HS-164, HS-48 (MEDIUM, R2; R10/R4 on some).** The core POC Stories (mic, voice detection, chip selection, first ANC test) are 43 days past their start and still To Do. Their Epics HS-1 and HS-2 have no owner. HS-164 and HS-48 sit in POC Sprint 3, which ended 2026-11-01. HS-144 also waits on the PCBs (now 2026-11-24, source risk), and HS-164 waits on the chip decision. Their windows run to the gate (2027-02-08), so nothing has slipped yet, but every week without a start uses up slack (inferred). EE, FW and ME wait.
- **HS-13, HS-381, HS-385, HS-422, HS-452, HS-457 (MEDIUM, R2).** The passive isolation, Zoo Meet USB/SDK, fast-charge and Bluetooth POC Stories have not started. No gate slip yet. HS-452 and HS-457 need the Headset board, which has no EE ticket (source risk). HS-326 (MEDIUM, R10) is In Process, but none of its 4 Subtasks has started.
- **Epics HS-1, HS-2, HS-3, HS-4, HS-51, HS-142, HS-380, HS-451 (MEDIUM, R2 + R4).** Start dates have passed and status is still To Do. This is a status issue with no gate impact on its own.

**Impact traces, source risks:**

- **Headset PCBs (HIGH; P1, P3, P4).** The paid order is stopped until about $35 extra is paid for the wrong board option (`transcripts/2026-10-19-biweekly.md · Pham Nguyen Ngoc 21:29–21:51`). No Jira ticket or comment up to 2026-11-03 says it was paid. The 3-week supplier quote (`2026-10-12-standup.md · Pham Nguyen Ngoc 26:00`) counted from today gives arrival about 2026-11-24, 46 working days after HS-144's start (2026-09-21). That is still before the POC gate, but HS-144 → HS-1, firmware HS-162 and the Bluetooth/USB Stories HS-452/HS-457 wait (inferred).
- **CTO demo (MEDIUM; S1, P5).** The visit started 2026-11-02 and no board has arrived, so no working Headset can be shown (inferred). No Jira ticket covers a demo build, and Jira records no visit outcome yet. No gate impact.
- **Chip choice (MEDIUM; S1).** HS-164, HS-154, HS-155, HS-162, HS-165 and HS-166 all assume NXP i.MX 95. Research has dropped XMOS and proposes the RK3576 (RS-1, RS-2). If that applies to the Headset, the PCB Rev 1 design, the board support package and the power budget all change. No decision is recorded in HS.
- **Arc scope (MEDIUM; S1).** The HS-79 result (2026-10-27) again misses the passive 8–12 dB target. HS-78, HS-89 and HS-93 (due 2026-12-01) continue against it.
- **EE tickets missing (MEDIUM; S1).** 15 days on, there is still no EE Subtask for the board order, bring-up, connectivity or ports, so their dates can't be checked.
- **Mechanical capacity (MEDIUM; S1).** Nguyen Trung Tin holds 8 open HS items. Two of his due dates moved out last week (HS-93 to 2026-12-01, HS-584 to 2026-11-16). Whether the Pro desk work caused this is not stated (inferred link only).

## 6. Recommendations for TM

1. **Release the Headset PCB order today.** Confirm the ~$35 difference is paid (the payment route was "ask Ms. Giang"). Get the supplier's ship date in writing, and log the order and its dates in an EE Subtask under HS-144. Each further week moves arrival by five working days (now 2026-11-24). Owners: Pham Nguyen Ngoc (requester), Cang Le.
2. **Record the chip decision from the CTO visit on HS-164.** Research has dropped XMOS and proposes the RK3576 (RS-2). Write the decision on HS-164 and update HS-154, HS-155, HS-162, HS-165 and HS-166, which all name the NXP i.MX 95. If the RK3576 is chosen, file the eval-kit purchase request the same week. Also check it against the "no components from China" rule in [[reference#Sourcing rules]] (the RS-2 comment says the CTO now accepts Chinese chips; not confirmed in an HS source). Owners: Cang Le, Minh Nguyen, Huynh Minh Tuan.
3. **Record the Arc scope decision.** HS-79 confirms that the passive Arc misses 8–12 dB. Decide during the visit whether to drop it, make it powered, or lower the target. Then close or re-scope HS-78, HS-89 and HS-93, and update HS-51. Owners: Cang Le, Nguyen Trung Tin.
4. **Get the EE Headset tickets created and the EE inputs owned.** It is 15 days since EE promised them. Create the board, bring-up, connectivity and ports Subtasks under HS-144 and HS-451. Assign HS-130, HS-131, HS-336 and HS-337, and have Mechanical check HS-335's model against the confirmed sizes. Owners: Pham Nguyen Ngoc, Nguyen Manh Tuan or Hung Pham Nguyen.
5. **Re-plan the sprints, assign owners and touch the stale tickets.** POC Sprint 3 ended 2026-11-01 with 51 tickets still in it, 482 open items have no assignee, and 504 open items are now stale. Move the open Sprint 3 tickets, start or assign owners for the Epics HS-1, HS-2 and HS-51, re-date HS-330 (4 days late), and extend HS-142 and HS-640 to 2027-09-06. Owners: Cang Le, Chico Ly (HS-142, HS-330), Phi Tuong (HS-640).

## 7. Data Gaps & Assumptions

- Snapshot `data/ZenaQuiet-Headset/2026-11-03.json`: 556 issues; field IDs: start date `customfield_10015`, sprint `customfield_10020`.
- **Jira pull:** the JQL `project = HS AND issuetype in (Epic, Story, Sub-task, Subtask) ORDER BY key ASC` returned a total of 556 (count query). I retrieved them in 6 pages of up to 100 (HS-1 → HS-647) using `nextPageToken`, all saved unchanged. `normalize` found 556 unique issues, equal to the JQL total (11 Epics, 106 Stories, 439 Subtasks). Every issue's comments came back complete (no `comment.total` larger than the number returned), so no `WORK/comments/` files were needed. Field IDs: start date `customfield_10015` and sprint `customfield_10020`, both confirmed in the data. Story → Epic and Subtask → Story use `parent`.
- **No change at all since 2026-11-02:** a field-by-field comparison of every issue with yesterday's pull found no difference, including "last updated" times and comments. All movement in this report comes from the date moving on by one day.
- **Numbers that moved for timing reasons, not progress:** stale items (R4) went from 99 to 504 and LOW risks from 77 to 481, because the 405 items last touched by the 2026-10-20 bulk title edit are now 14 days old (expected in the 2026-11-02 report). Expected progress went from 30.0% to 30.7%; days to gate from 98 to 97.
- **Unassigned:** no change. 482 of 516 open items have no assignee (7 Epics, 75 Stories, 400 Subtasks), listed in Appendix D. Team load for the Headset still can't be read from Jira.
- **Sprint dates:** the end date of POC Sprint 3 (2026-11-01) comes from earlier reports and the sprint objects; every sprint is still "future" in Jira.
- Sources: SOURCE RISKS: 6 items (P1 1 · P2 0 · P3 1 · P4 1 · P5 1 · S1 5) · HIGH 1 MEDIUM 5 LOW 0 · health with sources RED (Jira only AMBER) · lead times 14+3+5 wd · holidays 13 days
- 3 items worth a human look (not rule hits) are in Appendix C.
- **Source files:** no new source files arrived. Nothing in `raw/threads/` or `raw/transcripts/` is newer than 2026-10-19 (`threads/2026-10-19-purchase.md`; transcripts 2026-09-16, 2026-09-30, 2026-10-12, 2026-10-15, 2026-10-19, all read in earlier runs). The six Headset source items were carried over from the 2026-11-02 report, and every cited line was checked again in the raw files today (all found). Their status was updated from Jira comments: still no PCB payment, no HS chip decision, no Arc decision, no EE tickets, and nothing recorded from the CTO visit. Thread REQUEST 3 and REQUEST 4 are for Pro, and REQUEST 1 (GPU rental) names no product. No new purchase request was found in HS comments. The RK3576 eval-kit request lives in RS-2 and names no product, so it was not added as a Headset purchase.
- **Lead times:** the Headset PCB uses the supplier's "about three weeks" quote (`2026-10-12-standup.md · Pham Nguyen Ngoc 26:00`), counted from today because the supplier's restart date is unknown. That gives arrival 2026-11-24 (was 2026-11-23 on 2026-11-02). The approval date 2026-10-15 is matched to REQUEST 2 by inference (`2026-10-15-biweekly.md · Cang Le 25:15`, which says about $452 for the PCB, while the thread says about $600). The order-by date uses the default approval (14 wd) + Purchasing (3 wd) lead times. All needed-for links are inferred from titles. If the boards belong to HS-146 (EVT-1 boom array PCB) instead of HS-144, P1 and P4 go away.
- **Rule difference (script vs. INDEX):** the script tags every non-purchase source item S1, even when a ticket matches. All are kept as MEDIUM.
- **Contradictions (not resolved):** (1) Jira plans the NXP i.MX 95 for the Headset (HS-154, HS-155, HS-162, HS-164, HS-165, HS-166). Firmware proposed XMOS (`2026-10-19-biweekly.md · Minh Nguyen 07:37`), and Research has since dropped XMOS and proposed the RK3576 (RS-1, RS-2). (2) The RK3576 is a Chinese chip, which conflicts with the "No components from China (CTO)" rule in [[reference#Sourcing rules]]. RS-2 says the CTO now accepts Chinese chips; no HS source confirms this. (3) HS-79 is Done, but its comment says the target was not met. (4) HS-301 is Done, but its comment says it moves to EVT. (5) HS-86 and HS-127 are Done but titled "[TO BE REMOVED]". (6) PCB order state: the thread says "send to purchasing", but the 2026-10-19 meeting says it was paid. The newest source is used. (7) PCB cost: ~$600 in the thread vs about $452 in the 2026-10-15 meeting.
- **Not used:** Confluence. Epic descriptions were summarised, not copied.
